- **data**: Disciplined database write workflow. Every create / update / refactor of data goes through a Nitro task at &lt;layer&gt;/server/tasks/{seed,create,update,refactor}/&lt;noun&gt;.ts — never via raw SQL scripts, ad-hoc `nuxt db sql "INSERT ..."`, or one-off `tsx` scripts. Cleansing decisions are logged per defect class (fix at source / fix in flight / exclude / accept) with a business owner, and the transform is designed to be idempotent and re-runnable. - **data-acquisition**: Acquire the data a quantitative sociology analysis starts from — Dataverse replication packages, IPUMS/Census (ACS), GSS, ANES, ICPSR, OSF, and generic archives — and land it as immutable, provenanced raw data with a codebook and a re-runnable fetch script. Use at the very start of a quantitative project, before r-analyst / stata-analyst.
